# Mutation triage ledger

This is the one place where faults that no test catches are recorded (E41 decision 2, option B). Stryker never reads this file. Every fault listed here still counts against the score, so the floor in `scripts/mutation-baseline.json` sits below 100 to allow for them. Nothing is suppressed. How the runs work is in [mutation-testing.md](./mutation-testing.md).

## Classes

| Class             | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                | What happens                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Equivalent        | The changed code behaves the same for every input the function can receive                                                                                             | Recorded with a one-line proof. No test                                                                                                                                    |
| Not worth killing | Behaviour nobody relies on: log text, an unreachable guard, display copy whose only test would pin the exact wording, a loop made endless that only a timeout can stop | Recorded with a reason. The reviewer checks the reason                                                                                                                     |
| Timeout           | A test does fail on it, but Stryker reported a timeout first (a loaded machine, or a slow test reached it before the failing one)                                      | Recorded with the failing test. Stryker counts it as caught; the honest figure in mutation-testing.md does not                                                             |
| Tax gate          | Only a test that pins a tax rule still awaiting a ruling could catch it (the Scottish personal savings allowance band edge, E25)                                       | Logged, not tested, until the ruling. Tax figures in general are tested since the owner approved it on 19 November 2026, and the owner signs off each new figure in its PR |
| Possible bug      | Hand working disagrees with what the code returns                                                                                                                      | Reported to the coordinator. Engine source is not changed in M2                                                                                                            |

A real gap gets a test and no row here. Several faults on one source line with the same class and reason share a row. Line numbers are those of the source on the date given in each section; they drift as the code changes, so match on the fault and the source text as well.

## Engine outside tax and pay (E41 M2, PR A, 19 November 2026)

Measured by a forced full run on a laptop on 19 November 2026; `profile.ts` (after PR 808), `debt-payoff.ts` (after the review fixes) and the two housing selectors (after their last tests) were re-measured, and `debt-payoff.ts` again after the debt schedule fix after later changes the same day. Faults killed by a test have no row.

### `src/account-category.ts`

| Line | Fault                                                                    | Class      | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L26  | ConditionalExpression `false`; StringLiteral `""` (in place of `"owed"`) | Equivalent | No later check matches "owed", so it falls through to the final `return "other"`, the same result; an empty type also reaches `return "other"` either way. (A forced run on the laptop on 19 November showed both as Timeout once and Survived once: the timeouts were load, not a catch) |

### `src/anonymise.ts`

| Line      | Fault                                                                                                 | Class      | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L94       | StringLiteral `""` (regex escape replacement)                                                         | Equivalent | No scrub list entry contains a regex special character once spaces and hyphens are removed (checked over the whole list), so the escape never changes a pattern.                                                  |
| L99; L100 | MethodExpression (no sort); ArrowFunction `() => undefined`; ArithmeticOperator `b.length + a.length` | Equivalent | Order of alternatives only matters when one entry's letters are a prefix of another's; no entry is (checked over the whole list), and every alternative is bounded by `\b`, so every order matches the same text. |
| L152      | StringLiteral `""` ("Other")                                                                          | Equivalent | An unlisted type already falls back to ANONYMOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE "Other", so "other" maps to "Other" either way; the only new key, "", is returned early on L169.                                                    |
| L224      | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                         | Equivalent | When key is undefined, BUILT_IN_BUDGET_CATEGORY_LABELS[undefined] is undefined, the same as the guarded branch.                                                                                                   |

### `src/forecast.ts`

| Line                   | Fault                                                                                                                        | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L52                    | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]`                                                                                      | Equivalent        | The default only applies when no scenarios are given; a string element has no startYear, so `y >= undefined` and `y === undefined` are false and no cost is added                |
| L124                   | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `>= 0`                                                                        | Not worth killing | Differ only for a negative lumpSumCostGbp (at 0 the call is drawDown(0), a no-op); a lump sum is a cost and callers never pass a negative one                                    |
| L131                   | EqualityOperator `<= 0`                                                                                                      | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 the extra call is drawDown(0), which changes nothing                                                                                                                |
| L136                   | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `>= 0`                                                                        | Equivalent        | debtScenario is never below 0 (starts at max(0, ...), only grows or is floored at 0), and at 0 the paydown is min(0, x) = 0                                                      |
| L168; L169; L170; L171 | OptionalChaining (drop `?.`, four)                                                                                           | Not worth killing | trajectory[index] always exists for a whole, non-negative projectionYears; only a fractional or negative projectionYears, which callers never pass, reaches the `?? 0`           |
| L237                   | EqualityOperator `principal >= 0`                                                                                            | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L239                   | EqualityOperator `monthlyContribution >= 0`                                                                                  | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L241                   | EqualityOperator `monthlyRate >= 0`                                                                                          | Equivalent        | At rate 0 the loop gives principal + monthly * months, the same as the no-growth branch                                                                                          |
| L261                   | LogicalOperator `&&`; ConditionalExpression `false`; EqualityOperator `>= 0`; ConditionalExpression `true`                   | Equivalent        | futureValue already takes the no-growth path for a zero, negative or non-finite rate, so interest is max(0, 0) = 0 without this early return                                     |
| L269                   | EqualityOperator `balance >= 0`                                                                                              | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L271                   | EqualityOperator `monthlyContribution >= 0`                                                                                  | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L299                   | EqualityOperator `input.starting >= 0`                                                                                       | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L300                   | EqualityOperator `input.monthly >= 0`                                                                                        | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L303                   | ConditionalExpression `true` (col 45); EqualityOperator `>= 0`                                                               | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite still blocks NaN and Infinity; a negative rate then reaches only monthlyRateFromAer (0 for rate <= 0) and `rate <= 0` in flat, true for 0 and any negative alike |
| L340                   | EqualityOperator `input.starting >= 0`                                                                                       | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L342                   | EqualityOperator `input.monthly >= 0`                                                                                        | Equivalent        | At exactly 0 both branches give 0                                                                                                                                                |
| L344                   | ConditionalExpression `true` (col 5); LogicalOperator `\|\|`; EqualityOperator `>= 0`; ConditionalExpression `true` (col 45) | Equivalent        | rate is only passed to monthlyRateFromAer, which returns 0 for any non-finite, zero or negative AER                                                                              |

### `src/health-score.ts`

| Line | Fault                                                                | Class      | Reason                                                                                                                                         |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L41  | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `essentialBills >= 0` | Equivalent | `essentialBills` is `Math.max(1, ...)` on L37, so `essentialBills > 0` is always true                                                          |
| L72  | ConditionalExpression `false`                                        | Equivalent | At surplus 0 the else branch gives `Math.max(10, 50 - 0)` = 50, the same as this branch                                                        |
| L104 | ConditionalExpression `false`; EqualityOperator `runwayMonths > 6`   | Equivalent | At 6 months the next branch gives 80 + (3 / 3) * 20 = 100; above 6 it exceeds 100 and L110 caps it at 100                                      |
| L105 | EqualityOperator `runwayMonths > 3`                                  | Equivalent | At exactly 3 months both branches give 80 (80 + 0 and 3 / 3 * 80)                                                                              |
| L140 | ConditionalExpression `false`                                        | Equivalent | At debt 0 the formula gives `Math.max(0, 100 - 0)` = 100, the same as the branch                                                               |
| L183 | EqualityOperator `adjIncome >= taperEnd`                             | Equivalent | At exactly `taperEnd` the first branch on L178 (`<= taperEnd`) already matches, so this else-if is never reached with `adjIncome === taperEnd` |
| L220 | MethodExpression `observations` (no slice)                           | Equivalent | At most 3 observations are ever pushed (stage 1 up to 3, stages 2 and 3 up to 2), so `slice(0, 3)` never trims                                 |

| L139 | ConditionalExpression `typeof value === "number"` -> true | Equivalent        | `Number.isInteger` is false for every non-number, so dropping the typeof check changes nothing.                                           |
| L190 | StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"` (NoCo)                | Equivalent        | The mapped name is always a string already (`a.name ?? ""` on L165), so this fallback never runs.                                         |
| L209 | ConditionalExpression `payment === null` -> false         | Equivalent        | `payment` is a number or null, and `null <= 0` is true in JavaScript, so `payment <= 0` alone already returns null for a missing payment. |
| L243 | ArrayDeclaration `[]`; StringLiteral `""` (x2)            | Equivalent        | `withLinkedBudgetRows` reads only `takeHome.status` ("unknown" either way), never the missing list or the reason.                         |
| L285 | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]`                   | Equivalent        | `rentAtShare` looks for a row with `linkKind: "housing"`; a string has none, so the saved rent comes back unchanged, as with `[]`.        |
| L311 | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]`                   | Not worth killing | Only differs for an account whose id is that exact string; no real id is.                                                                 |
| L317 | ConditionalExpression `birthYear !== null` -> true        | Equivalent        | `null > 0` is false, so a missing birth year still gives no age.                                                                          |
| L425 | OptionalChaining `data.baseline.housingStatus` (NoCo)     | Equivalent        | `baseline` is required by `BuildContextPackInput`, so it is never undefined for a typed caller.                                           |

### `src/retirement.ts`

| Line                                | Fault                                                                                                | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L89, L92, L169, L1019, L1022, L1025 | StringLiteral `""` (RangeError messages)                                                             | Not worth killing | Developer-facing error text; callers and tests rely on the RangeError type, which the new tests assert                                                                                                                                   |
| L106                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (the `!== undefined` part)                                              | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite(undefined) is false, so an absent rate still resolves to null                                                                                                                                                            |
| L171                                | ConditionalExpression `false` (1 + r === 0 branch)                                                   | Equivalent        | When 1 + r is 0 the other arm gives 0 ** (1/12) - 1 = -1, the same value                                                                                                                                                                 |
| L178                                | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                        | Not worth killing | Zero-contribution whole-year pots then go through the monthly loop at (1 + r)^(1/12), which compounds to the same balance up to floating-point rounding (it can differ by a penny at extreme balances), so it is not strictly equivalent |
| L261                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (the typeof part)                                                       | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite rejects every non-number, so the typeof check is redundant                                                                                                                                                               |
| L268                                | StringLiteral `""` (frequency default)                                                               | Equivalent        | budgetRowMonthly treats any frequency other than weekly or annual as monthly, so "" and "monthly" give the same amount                                                                                                                   |
| L304                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (the typeof part)                                                       | Equivalent        | "stops" and "continues" return earlier; any other non-object value has no `kind` of "changes", so the line is skipped as before (null is still caught by the `!== null` check)                                                           |
| L306                                | EqualityOperator `changed >= 0`                                                                      | Equivalent        | Adding a changed amount of exactly 0 leaves the total unchanged                                                                                                                                                                          |
| L447                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (x2, the Number.isFinite parts); LogicalOperator `(A && B \|\| C) && D` | Equivalent        | calculateMonthlyMortgagePayment returns 0 for a principal of 0, below 0 or NaN and for a non-finite rate, so the payment > 0 check sends these to the same fallback                                                                      |
| L447                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (balance > 0); EqualityOperator `balance >= 0`                          | Equivalent        | Same reason: a principal of 0 or below gives a payment of 0 and the same fallback                                                                                                                                                        |
| L447                                | LogicalOperator `isFinite(balance) \|\| balance > 0`                                                 | Not worth killing | Only differs for an infinite balance at 0%, which saved mortgage data cannot hold                                                                                                                                                        |
| L460                                | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `explicitPayment >= 0`                                | Equivalent        | With balance > 0 and rate >= 0, a payment of 0 or below always meets `payment <= balance * monthlyRate` on L465 and returns null, as the original does                                                                                   |
| L471                                | EqualityOperator `bal >= 0.01`                                                                       | Not worth killing | Only differs when the balance lands on exactly 1p, which adds one month to a mortgage term                                                                                                                                               |
| L488                                | ArithmeticOperator `retirementAge - 1`                                                               | Equivalent        | planToAge is only read for retirementYears, which is clamped to at least 1 on L489, so both give 1 year when planToAge <= retirementAge                                                                                                  |
| L494                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (typeof check alone)                                                    | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite already rejects every non-number, so dropping the typeof check changes nothing                                                                                                                                           |
| L541                                | OptionalChaining `yearlySchedule[0].annualCostGbp`; ArithmeticOperator `baseMonthly / 12` (NoCo)     | Equivalent        | retirementYears is at least 1, so yearlySchedule[0] always exists and the fallback is unreachable                                                                                                                                        |
| L609                                | OptionalChaining `yearlySchedule[0].annualCostGbp`                                                   | Equivalent        | Same as L541: the schedule always has at least one year                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| L613                                | EqualityOperator `monthsInRetirement >= 0`; ConditionalExpression `true`                             | Equivalent        | buildMortgageContinuesResult is only called with monthsInRetirement > 0 (L644 and L673 return earlier otherwise)                                                                                                                         |
| L642                                | ConditionalExpression `true`                                                                         | Equivalent        | The override union has five kinds and the other four return earlier, so only mortgage-continues reaches this check                                                                                                                       |
| L741, L748                          | ConditionalExpression `true` (the typeof part)                                                       | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite rejects every non-number, so the typeof check is redundant; the other mutants on these lines are killed by the zero, negative and infinite tests                                                                         |
| L763                                | ConditionalExpression `true` (typeof part)                                                           | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite(undefined) is false, so the result is the same                                                                                                                                                                           |
| L1004                               | EqualityOperator `potGbp >= 0`                                                                       | Equivalent        | A pot of exactly 0 gives 0 from either branch (0 / years or 0 * r / ...)                                                                                                                                                                 |
| L1005                               | EqualityOperator `Math.abs(realAnnualRate) <= 1e-12`                                                 | Equivalent        | At a rate of exactly 1e-12 the annuity formula and pot / years agree to far below a penny                                                                                                                                                |
| L1072, L1073, L1074                 | ArrayDeclaration `new Array()` (pathPot)                                                             | Equivalent        | Every read is `pathPot[c][y] ?? 0` and every write assigns by index, so an empty start array gives the same values                                                                                                                       |
| L1087                               | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                        | Equivalent        | At y = 0 the other arm reads yearEndContributions[-1], which is undefined and becomes 0                                                                                                                                                  |
| L1124                               | ConditionalExpression `true` (the typeof part)                                                       | Equivalent        | Number.isFinite rejects every non-number, so the typeof check is redundant                                                                                                                                                               |
| L1153                               | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `extraPensionMonthly >= 0`                            | Equivalent        | Projecting a pot of £0 with £0 a month adds 0 to every total and the path                                                                                                                                                                |
| L1164                               | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `extraSavingsMonthly >= 0`                            | Equivalent        | Same as L1153: a £0 savings projection adds nothing                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| L1255                               | OptionalChaining `housing.yearlySchedule[i].annualCostGbp`                                           | Equivalent        | The housing schedule has planToAge - retirementAge entries, the same as the loop, so index i always exists                                                                                                                               |
| L1291                               | ConditionalExpression `true`                                                                         | Equivalent        | statePension is only non-null when isStatePensionIncluded holds, which requires statePensionAge !== null                                                                                                                                 |
| L1381                               | StringLiteral `""` (type default "Pension")                                                          | Not worth killing | Only reached for an untyped account the user marked as counting; the type then only changes whether a joint share applies. Worth an owner look: an untyped joint account is read as a pension and counted in full                        |
| L1383, L1384, L1385                 | ConditionalExpression `true`                                                                         | Equivalent        | Passing `name: undefined` (or ownership, sharePercent) is read the same as leaving the key out                                                                                                                                           |
| L1388                               | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                        | Equivalent        | selectNetWorthSeries with no accounts ignores every row and returns [] (first stays infinite)                                                                                                                                            |
| L1393                               | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `idx >= 0`                                            | Equivalent        | At idx 0, points[-1] is undefined, the same as the original's undefined                                                                                                                                                                  |

| Line       | Fault                                                                               | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L132       | StringLiteral `""` (a.type ?? "other")                                              | Equivalent        | categorizeAccountType maps "" and "other" both to "other", and neither is an ISA or pension for the share rules                                                |

| L166       | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                       | Equivalent        | with no rows first is +Infinity, the month loop never runs (Infinity <= end is false) and [] is returned                                                       |
| L168       | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                       | Equivalent        | parseMonth(undefined) tests the string "undefined", fails, and returns null                                                                                    |
| L169       | EqualityOperator `through >= last`; ConditionalExpression `true` (through !== null) | Equivalent        | through === last gives end = last either way; null > last is false as last >= key(1900, 1) > 0                                                                 |
| L170       | ConditionalExpression `true` (through !== null)                                     | Equivalent        | null >= last is false (last > 0), so balancesAt stays null                                                                                                     |
| L174       | ConditionalExpression `true` (balancesAt !== null)                                  | Equivalent        | with balancesAt null the saved-balance ids join idSet but `idx === balancesAt` never holds, so they never get a value and are skipped at `value === undefined` |
| L212       | EqualityOperator `value > 0`                                                        | Equivalent        | a 0 asset then adds -0 to liabilities instead of 0 to assets; 0 + -0 is 0, so every total is the same                                                          |

## Engine outside tax and pay: added after the part B run (19 November 2026)

The forced full run for part B, on a heavily loaded laptop, reported some faults in the part A files that the part A runs had counted as timeouts or killed: endless loops that only a timeout can stop, and faults that a test does catch but that timed out first. They are recorded here so that every fault the honest figure counts as not caught has a row.

| L189 | UpdateOperator `m--`     | Not worth killing | Makes the loop `for (let m = 1; m <= 60; m++) {` never end, so the only possible catch is a timeout (Stryker reports one); a timeout is not counted as caught in the honest figure                 |
| L268 | BlockStatement `{}`      | Not worth killing | Makes the loop `while (month < maxMonths) {` never end, so the only possible catch is a timeout (Stryker reports one); a timeout is not counted as caught in the honest figure                     |

## Tax and pay (E41 M2, PR B, 19 November 2026)

Measured by a forced full run on a laptop on 19 November 2026, after PRs 807, 808 and 810. A fault Stryker reported as a timeout, or as killed only because a self-timing test ran slow, counts as not caught here unless an earlier run on the same code saw a real assertion fail on it. Faults killed by a test have no row.

The `uk-take-home.ts` row was removed when PR 824 fixed the Plan 1 and Plan 2 split; that PR also added about 15 lines, so later line numbers in that file are about 15 higher.

### `src/uk-tax.ts` (tax and pay)

| Line                               | Fault                                                                                                                 | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L123:36                            | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `taxable >= b.from`                                                    | Equivalent        | Bands are ascending and contiguous and find returns the first match: the lower bands fail `taxable <= b.to`, and at taxable == b.from the band below matches first, so the same band is returned.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| L123:57                            | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                                         | Equivalent        | Only the top band has to === null; with the test false no band matches above the top, and the fallback `bands.at(-1)?.rate` returns the same top-band rate.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| L124                               | OptionalChaining `band.rate`; OptionalChaining `bands.at(-1).rate`; UnaryOperator `+1`                                | Not worth killing | The fallback is unreachable: taxable > 0 always falls in some band because the top band has no upper limit; `band` is never undefined.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| L165                               | BlockStatement `{}`                                                                                                   | Equivalent        | Returning undefined makes callers pass taxYear undefined to resolveUkTaxYear, which resolves today's year exactly as resolveUkTaxYear() does.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| L196; L271; L292; L330; L401; L538 | StringLiteral `""` (region default or fixed region)                                                                   | Equivalent        | Region is only ever compared with "SCOTLAND" (marginalIncomeTaxRate, calculateIncomeTaxOnTaxable, take-home core), so "" behaves as England, Wales and Northern Ireland.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| L238                               | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `excessOver100k >= 0`                                                  | Equivalent        | With excessOver100k = 0 the expression is NI(niPay) - NI(niPay - 0) = 0, the same as the 0 branch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| L364; L407                         | StringLiteral `""` (plan "NONE")                                                                                      | Equivalent        | toStudentLoanPlanKey("") and toStudentLoanPlanKey("NONE") both return undefined (no such write-off key), so no plan is charged.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| L380                               | MethodExpression `taxYear`                                                                                            | Equivalent        | Number.parseInt("2025/26") stops at "/" and gives 2025, the same as parsing the first four characters.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| L418                               | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `pensionPct >= 0`                                                      | Equivalent        | A pension of 0% gives a contribution of 0 under every method, so every figure calculateUkTax returns is the same as with no pension.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| L419                               | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]`                                                                               | Equivalent        | studentLoanRepayments keeps only known undergraduate plans and postgrad, so an unknown id charges nothing; the rule-key list it touches is not returned by calculateUkTax.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| L442; L450; L451                   | ConditionalExpression `false` (L442:5, L450:5); StringLiteral `""` (L442:16, L450:16); EqualityOperator `>=` (L451:9) | Tax gate          | These only change the Scottish personal savings allowance band. Without a band extension the Scottish and EWNI edges agree, so a test needs Gift Aid or relief at source for a Scottish taxpayer, or the 50,270 Scottish higher edge itself: both are the open accountant ruling (E25 OWNER STEPS 2, marked UNVERIFIED in the code); asserting them would pin an undecided rule (AGENTS.md s.13). |

## Contract, MCP and small packages (E41 M3, 19 November 2026)

Measured by forced runs on a laptop on 19 November 2026 after the kill tests. Faults killed by a test have no row. Timeouts count as not caught. Runtime errors (the mutated module fails to load) are left out of the score by Stryker and have no row. Line numbers are those of the measured source.

One more class appears here. Timed out under load: a test killed the fault in an earlier run, and it timed out in the final run on a loaded machine. It counts as not caught until a calmer run kills it again.

### `packages/api-contract`

1,255 faults: 1,163 killed, 0 timed out, 68 survived, 6 no coverage, 18 runtime errors (not scored). Re-measured by a forced run on 20 November 2026, after `main` changed `src/domains/mcp-tokens.ts`, `src/domains/profile.ts` and `src/domains/tax.ts` (retired profile fields removed, tax category lists added). Line numbers are those of that run's source. Three faults in `taxCategoryError` (`tax.ts` L20, L114, L115: the legacy salary id and the trim) are real gaps reported for a test, so they have no row.

Not worth killing for want of a documented rule (the bound is written only in the schema; coordinator to decide): avatar https URL length 2048 (`profile.ts` L15), validation message cap of 200 characters (`validate.ts` L34), the float tolerance in the decimal-place checks (`primitives.ts` L6, L49).

### `packages/mcp`

4,212 faults: 3,893 killed, 91 timed out, 188 survived, 34 no coverage, 6 runtime errors (not scored). Four lanes triaged their own files from runs over those files alone; this is one forced run over the whole package afterwards, on a loaded laptop. Each fault took longer, so 72 faults that an earlier run killed timed out here (class Timed out under load). `src/provider/in-memory.ts` and `src/tools/read/common.ts` were re-measured by a forced run on 20 November 2026 after their tests changed: no new fault survives, and the 23 faults there that had timed out under load were killed, so their rows are gone. The fault in `src/auth/token-verifier.ts` that a lane reported to the coordinator (a token accepted at its exact expiry instant) was fixed and is now killed.

### `packages/banking`

1,203 faults: 1,129 killed, 8 timed out, 60 survived, 6 no coverage, 0 runtime errors. `src/server/adapters/csv.ts` was re-measured by a forced run on 20 November 2026, after a test that pinned the output for malformed dates was removed (seven more faults on L242 survive). The credit card amount owed that was not rounded to pence, found here, was fixed in PR 818.

| L5   | Regex `sort.code` (in place of `sort.?code`)                                           | Equivalent        | Any key containing "sortcode" also contains "code", which is its own entry in the same pattern, so every key still matches                                                                                                                     |
| L11  | MethodExpression `text` (no slice to MAX_STRING_LENGTH)                                | Not worth killing | The slice is a cost cap; the only difference is that the fault also finds an email after 8192 characters, which is stricter, and the bounded email pattern is linear either way                                                                |
| L121 | LogicalOperator `\|\|`; ConditionalExpression `true`; UpdateOperator `end++` (Timeout) | Not worth killing | With no boundary before the limit the loop never ends: `end` falls below 0, or with `end++` runs past the end of the text, where `charAt` gives "" and no boundary. Only a time limit stops it, and Stryker's timeout is not counted as caught |
| L171 | EqualityOperator `depth > MAX_DEPTH`; EqualityOperator `state.nodes >= MAX_NODES`      | Not worth killing | Off by one in the walk's cost caps: one level or node more or less is walked, and every string and sensitive key in it is still redacted, so nothing leaves unredacted; a test would only pin the private constants                            |

#### `src/scrub-event.ts`

| Line             | Fault                                                                     | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| L40              | StringLiteral `""` (in place of `"version"`)                              | Equivalent        | "version" never matches the sensitive key pattern, so whether it is a safe software key changes nothing                                                            |
| L46              | ConditionalExpression `true`                                              | Not worth killing | Writes keys whose value is undefined; they vanish when the event is serialised to JSON for sending, so nothing different reaches Sentry                            |
| L57              | Regex `/[?#].*/s` (no `$`)                                                | Equivalent        | `.*` with the s flag runs to the end of the string with or without `$`                                                                                             |
| L61              | Regex without `$`                                                         | Equivalent        | The last group `(.*)` with the s flag always runs to the end of the string                                                                                         |
| L67              | EqualityOperator `i <= 2`; UpdateOperator `i--`                           | Not worth killing | Both decode one or more extra levels, which is stricter (a deeper encoded @ is also redacted); the function documents two levels (%40 and %2540)                   |
| L78              | ConditionalExpression `true` (first operand)                              | Not worth killing | A segment with a partial match is redacted whole instead of in part: stricter, nothing leaks, only the shape of an already-redacted segment changes                |
| L99              | StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"` (three, NoCo, destructuring defaults) | Equivalent        | All three groups of URL_PARTS take part in every match (the last can be empty but not undefined), so the defaults are never used                                   |
| L100             | Regex `/.*@/s` (no `^`)                                                   | Equivalent        | `.*` can start at position 0, so the leftmost match is the same with or without the anchor                                                                         |
| L121             | Regex `/(\s)/`; Regex `/(\S+)/`                                           | Equivalent        | With the separator captured, the pieces are the same whitespace and non-whitespace runs (or empty strings, which map to themselves) and join back to the same text |
| L138             | Regex `/file:\/\//i` (no `^`)                                             | Not worth killing | Differs only when "file://" appears in the middle of a frame path, which runtimes do not produce                                                                   |
| L139; L140       | Regex without `^`                                                         | Equivalent        | `.*` and `.*?` can start at position 0, so the leftmost match and the replaced text are the same                                                                   |
| L249             | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]`                                   | Equivalent        | The fallback is filtered with isObject, and a string is not an object, so the values are [] either way                                                             |
| L276             | StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"`                                       | Equivalent        | Neither "" nor the stand-in is a network category, so the breadcrumb is dropped either way                                                                         |
| L281; L283; L291 | ConditionalExpression `true` (first operand)                              | Equivalent        | The second operand already refuses a non-string or non-number: `/^[A-Z]+$/.test(undefined)` tests "undefined", which fails, and Number.isFinite does not coerce    |
| L300             | ConditionalExpression `false`                                             | Equivalent        | A non-object crumb either throws on property access (null, undefined), which the catch turns into null, or has no string category, which returns null              |

| Line | Fault                                                                          | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L81  | Regex `/v/i` (unanchored)                                                      | Not worth killing | Differs only for a "v" inside the numeric core (for example "1.v2.0"), which is not a version format any client sends; a leading "v" and a "-dev" suffix give the same result                                                                                                     |
| L81  | StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"` in `?? ""` (NoCo)                          | Equivalent        | `split` always returns at least one element, so `[0]` is never undefined and the fallback is unreachable                                                                                                                                                                          |
| L93  | EqualityOperator `i <= len`                                                    | Equivalent        | The extra index is past both arrays, so `(a[i] ?? 0) - (b[i] ?? 0)` is 0 and the loop goes on to `return false`, the same result                                                                                                                                                  |
| L93  | UpdateOperator `i--` (Timeout)                                                 | Not worth killing | An endless loop when the leading parts are equal (for example 1.0.0 against 1.0.0): `i` falls below 0 and stays under `len`. `isBelowMin` is synchronous and takes no callback, so only a time limit can stop it; Stryker reports it as a timeout, which is not counted as caught |
| L95  | EqualityOperator `diff <= 0`                                                   | Equivalent        | Reached only when `diff !== 0`, where `diff <= 0` and `diff < 0` agree                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| L135 | OptionalChaining `signal.reason`                                               | Equivalent        | `onAbort` is only called or registered when `signal` is defined                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| L138 | StringLiteral `""` (event name in `removeEventListener`)                       | Not worth killing | The listener left on the caller's signal can only call `finish` on a promise that has already settled: a second reject is ignored, so nothing observable changes                                                                                                                  |
| L142 | ObjectLiteral `{}`; BooleanLiteral `false` (`once: true`)                      | Equivalent        | An AbortSignal dispatches "abort" at most once, and `finish` removes the listener anyway                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| L149 | ArrowFunction `() => undefined` (rejection handler)                            | Equivalent        | `refreshOnce` returns a promise with `.catch(() => "retryable")`, so it never rejects and the handler never runs                                                                                                                                                                  |
| L150 | ArrowFunction `() => undefined`; StringLiteral `""`; ObjectLiteral `{}` (NoCo) | Equivalent        | Inside the same unreachable rejection handler as L149                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

#### `src/transport.ts`

| ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L160       | ConditionalExpression `false`; MethodExpression `text` (no trim); StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"`                         | Equivalent        | An empty or whitespace text makes `JSON.parse` throw, and the catch sets `body = null`, the same as the `null` branch                                                                                                           |

| L238       | StringLiteral `""` (timeout reason `new Error("timeout")`)                                                                    | Not worth killing | Internal abort reason text: it reaches callers only as the message of a `cause`, which nothing reads                                                                                                                            |
| L261       | ConditionalExpression `false`                                                                                                 | Equivalent        | `JSON.stringify(undefined)` is `undefined`, the same as the other branch                                                                                                                                                        |
| L283; L284 | ConditionalExpression `true`                                                                                                  | Equivalent        | `requestId` and `issuePaths` are declared class fields, already undefined; assigning undefined changes nothing                                                                                                                  |
| L289       | ConditionalExpression `true`                                                                                                  | Equivalent        | A 204 Response has a null body, so `res.text()` gives "", the value `text` already has                                                                                                                                          |
| L317       | StringLiteral `""` (event name in `removeEventListener`)                                                                      | Not worth killing | The listener left on the caller's signal calls `abort` on a controller whose call has finished; nothing observable changes                                                                                                      |
| L333       | EqualityOperator `round <= MAX_STRIP_ROUNDS`                                                                                  | Not worth killing | One extra strip round on a schema that never converges still ends in the same failure; the only test would pin the private round limit                                                                                          |
| L335       | ConditionalExpression `false` (`issues.length === 0`)                                                                         | Equivalent        | With no issues, `every` on an empty list is true, so nothing is stripped, the reparse fails the same way and the loop returns that same failure                                                                                 |
| L335       | LogicalOperator `i.code === "unrecognized_keys" \|\| i.keys`; ConditionalExpression `true` (`i.code === "unrecognized_keys"`) | Equivalent        | Only `unrecognized_keys` issues carry `keys`, and one without keys strips nothing, so the loop returns the same failure                                                                                                         |
| L343       | ConditionalExpression `true` (three); LogicalOperator `\|\|`                                                                  | Not worth killing | An `unrecognized_keys` issue's path always leads through objects to the object that holds the keys, so the walk never meets null or a primitive; the guard only protects against a schema whose issue path contradicts the body |
| L347       | ConditionalExpression `false` (three); LogicalOperator `&&`                                                                   | Not worth killing | Same as L343: the target of an `unrecognized_keys` issue is always an object                                                                                                                                                    |
| L348       | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]` (NoCo)                                                                                | Equivalent        | L335 lets only issues with `keys` through, so `?? []` never applies                                                                                                                                                             |
| L378       | OptionalChaining `last.status`                                                                                                | Equivalent        | MAX_PAGES is 100, so the loop runs at least once and `last` is always set here                                                                                                                                                  |
| L379       | ConditionalExpression `true`; OptionalChaining `last.requestId`                                                               | Equivalent        | `last` is always set (L378), and `requestId` is a declared class field, so assigning undefined changes nothing                                                                                                                  |

## Backend guards, auth, security, contract and services (E41 M4, 20 November 2026)

Measured on a laptop on 20 November 2026 with the unit Vitest project only (owner decision 11: database specs are left out of mutation runs at first). Line numbers are those of the measured source. Faults killed by a test have no row, and timeouts count as not caught.

Two kinds of fault that no unit test catches are recorded here:

- Faults that a database spec catches. A second laptop run added the database specs (a throwaway Postgres in Docker, each Stryker worker on its own database); the 14 files main changed during M4 were measured again the same way after the rebase. Writing unit tests for these would only repeat the database specs, so they are counted per file below, not triaged row by row. "Killed" means a database spec failed on the fault. "Errored" means the database run stopped with an error or timed out on it, which Stryker leaves out of the score: likely caught, but not proved.

- Faults that neither run catches. These were triaged; each one left has a row in the area tables below.

Main changed or added 14 of these files during M4 (the beta invite module, budget shares, agent changes). Their rows are in the last area, "Files main changed or added during M4", against the rebased source; the earlier areas' summary tables still count those files as first triaged.

| L76  | StringLiteral `""` ("missing sub")                                          | Not worth killing | Internal error text, caught and replaced by "invalid token".                                                                                                                                                   |

| L85  | ConditionalExpression `true`, LogicalOperator `\|\|`                        | Equivalent        | `Number.isFinite` is false for every non-number, and a JSON number is always finite, so `typeof x === "number" && Number.isFinite(x)`, `Number.isFinite(x)` and the `\|\|` form agree for every decoded claim. |

#### `src/security/client-key.ts`

Rewritten in this slice (one key per address, however it is written), so these rows replace the triage of the old code and their line numbers are those of the new source. Every row is defensive code after `isIPv6` has accepted the address: the WHATWG URL parser accepts every address `isIPv6` accepts and writes it as eight hex groups or with one `::`.

| Line | Fault                                                                            | Class      | Reason                                                                                                                                 |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L21  | ConditionalExpression `false` (`zone === 0`)                                     | Equivalent | A leading `%` leaves the address part empty, and `isIPv6("")` is false, so the same condition still returns "invalid"                  |
| L24  | ConditionalExpression `false` (`!groups`)                                        | Equivalent | expandIPv6 returns null only when the URL parser refuses the text, and it accepts every address `isIPv6` has accepted                  |
| L44  | BlockStatement `{}` (the catch)                                                  | Equivalent | Unreachable for the same reason: the URL parser never throws on an address `isIPv6` accepted (no coverage)                             |
| L47  | StringLiteral `"Stryker was here!"` (both defaults)                              | Equivalent | `split("::")` always gives a first part, and the tail default is read only when there is no `::`, where tailGroups is empty either way |
| L49  | StringLiteral `""` (in `includes("::")`)                                         | Equivalent | `includes("")` is always true; without `::` the tail is the empty default, so `true && ""` still gives no tail groups                  |
| L52  | ConditionalExpression `true` (x5); LogicalOperator (x2); MethodExpression `some` | Equivalent | The canonical form always has eight groups, each 0 to ffff, so the validity check is always true                                       |

| Line          | Fault                                          | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                            |
| ------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L33           | StringLiteral dev root `""`                    | Not worth killing | Changes only development and test keys; a fixed public root of any value meets the rule, and the only test would pin the constant |
| L66, L68, L69 | StringLiteral (logger context, message text)   | Not worth killing | Log text; the existing test checks the line names the variable and holds no values                                                |
| L90           | ConditionalExpression `false` (skip the cache) | Equivalent        | The derived key is recomputed from the same root and label, giving the same bytes; only speed changes                             |
| L92           | ObjectLiteral `{}` cached                      | Equivalent        | The cache entry never matches, so the key is recomputed each time with the same result                                            |

| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| L142, L143         | BlockStatement `{}` in the sweep catch; StringLiteral log text | Not worth killing | Log line only; the sweep swallows the error either way                                                                                                                    |
| L169               | EqualityOperator `retry >= blocked.retry`                      | Not worth killing | Differs only when two refusing windows have the same wait; the wait reported is the same, only which window is named as `exceeded` changes, and either meets the contract |

| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L55  | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `!== 0`; UpdateOperator `--this.hits` | Not worth killing | Change only how often the background sweep runs; a sweep drops only buckets every reader already treats as expired, so decisions never change. The every-500 test still catches "never sweeps"             |
| L66  | EqualityOperator `retryMs >= blocked.retryMs`                                        | Not worth killing | As pg L169: only the `exceeded` window on an exact tie changes; the wait is the same                                                                                                                       |
| L109 | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `now <= bucket.resetAt`               | Equivalent        | Differ only for a refund at or after the window end; that bucket is already expired for `hit`, `peek` and `sweep` (all use `now >= resetAt`), so the changed count is never read on a forward-moving clock |
| L114 | ConditionalExpression `false` (makeRoom always runs)                                 | Equivalent        | Under the cap the eviction loop exits at once, and the extra sweep drops only expired buckets                                                                                                              |

| L78      | ConditionalExpression `true` (no length check) | Equivalent | The format accepts exactly 64 hex characters, which decode to 32 bytes, the length of a SHA-256 digest, so the lengths always match |

| Line | Fault                                            | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L71  | StringLiteral `""` (object entry joiner)         | Equivalent        | Each entry starts with a JSON-quoted key, which ends at an unescaped quote, so entries joined with nothing still decode one way: distinct arguments keep distinct canonical text, and a fingerprint is only ever compared with one made by the same function. |

## Web app and UI logic (E41 M5, 21 November 2026)

Measured by forced full runs on commit 66c6fe131 (the configs before any kill test): `apps/web` 6,836 faults, `packages/ui` 8,496, string faults left out in both (decision 7). Eight areas were triaged, each by its own agent; a fault killed by a new test has no row. Kills were confirmed by single-file Stryker runs, or, where an agent's last tests came after its last run, by applying the fault by hand and seeing the test fail; each area says which. Line numbers are those of commit 66c6fe131. In each area's summary the class counts are faults unless the area says it counts rows.

No survivor showed a bug in source. Two notes for later work, neither a wrong figure: `CurrencyInput`'s comment says an undefined value shows an empty field, but the hook deliberately leaves the text alone when `value` is undefined (callers use `null` for empty); and `StudentLoanCalculator`'s refusal line has no live region, so screen readers do not hear it.

#### `app/utils/demoData.ts`

#### `app/utils/yAxisTicks.ts`

A script in the session scratchpad compared the original with each remaining fault over about 50,000 value pairs (integer pairs from -5,000 to 5,000 and scaled pairs up to ±340,000).

| Line | Fault                                                                      | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                         |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L36  | ConditionalExpression `false`; EqualityOperator `min > 0`, `lo <= 0`       | Equivalent        | With `min >= 0`, `lo = floor(min / pad) x pad` is never negative, so `lo < 0` never holds and setting `lo = 0` when `lo` is 0 changes nothing  |
| L39  | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `min >= 0` (second operand) | Equivalent        | `lo` is 0 only when 0 <= min < pad; the extra case min = 0 gives `max(0, floor(-pad/4 / grid) x grid)` = 0, the same `lo`                      |
| L44  | EqualityOperator `v < hi + step / 1e6`                                     | Equivalent        | `v` is a multiple of `step` and `hi` a multiple of `step / 4`, so `v` never equals `hi + step / 1e6` exactly                                   |
| L47  | ConditionalExpression `false`                                              | Not worth killing | Differs only when `lo` is -0, which needs a -0 data value; -0 and 0 draw the same axis                                                         |
| L49  | EqualityOperator `ticks.length < MAX_Y_TICKS`                              | Equivalent        | A 4-tick axis has already returned at L48                                                                                                      |
| L49  | ConditionalExpression `true` (first operand, allows 1-tick fallbacks)      | Not worth killing | No input in the search made any division give fewer than 2 ticks while none gave 3 or 4                                                        |
| L50  | ConditionalExpression `false` (second operand, NoCo)                       | Equivalent        | 3- and 4-tick axes return at L48, so the fallback and every later candidate have 2 ticks and `>` is never true                                 |
| L50  | ConditionalExpression `true`; EqualityOperator `>=`, `<=` (NoCo)           | Not worth killing | These keep the last 2-tick axis instead of the first; no input in the search had two divisions with different 2-tick axes and none with 3 or 4 |
| L53  | ObjectLiteral `{}`; ArrayDeclaration `[]` twice (NoCo)                     | Not worth killing | The last-resort axis was not reached by any input in the search; flat series are widened first and division 3 always gave 2 to 4 ticks         |

### Web hooks and services (E41 M5, 21 November 2026)

Measured from the forced full run on commit 66c6fe131 (`web-mutation.json`), then each file re-run on its own with `--force --concurrency 2` after the new tests. Area: every file under `app/hooks/` and `app/services/` in `apps/web`. Faults killed by a new test have no row.

Line numbers are the report's. In a file that starts with `// @ts-nocheck` (marked † below) the report's source omits that line, so the line in the file is one higher; match on the fault and the source text as well.

Counts in this table are faults, not rows.

Two kills in `backend-client.server.ts` (L153: LogicalOperator `&&`, and ConditionalExpression `false` on `shared === undefined`) came from a test change made after that file's last Stryker run; they were confirmed by applying each fault by hand and seeing the test fail, not by a further Stryker run.

Why so many survived in `useUserProfile.ts`: every existing test file for that hook loads it with `await import("./useUserProfile")`. Stryker runs with `vitest.related: true`, which finds related tests through static imports, so those files did not run against its faults. The new `useUserProfile.versions.test.tsx` imports the hook statically. Converting the older files to static imports would let Stryker count them too; that is a follow-up, not done here.

| L3   | ArrowFunction `() => undefined` (the subscribe's unsubscribe) | Equivalent | React calls the returned clean-up only if it is a function; the store never changes, so there is nothing to unsubscribe from either way |

#### `app/services/api-proxy.server.ts`

| Line | Fault                                                                                                | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L126 | OptionalChaining `findRule(method, path).query`                                                      | Not worth killing | The only caller (`handleApiProxy`, L285) runs it after `matchAllowList` has found a rule, so `findRule` is never undefined there; only a direct call with an unlisted path could throw |
| L212 | ConditionalExpression `false` (`path === null`)                                                      | Equivalent        | `matchAllowList(method, null)` tests the regexes against the text "null", which no rule matches, so the request is still a 404                                                         |
