| L245 | ConditionalExpression `true` (twice); LogicalOperator `\|\|`; ConditionalExpression `true` at col 27 | Equivalent        | A GET or HEAD Request cannot carry a body, so reading it gives "" and `body` stays undefined; no rule allows HEAD, so it is a 404 before this line                                     |
| L290 | ObjectLiteral `{}`; BooleanLiteral `false` (`once`)                                                  | Equivalent        | An AbortSignal fires `abort` once only, and the listener is removed in `finally`                                                                                                       |
| L304 | ObjectLiteral `{}` (`{ cause }`)                                                                     | Equivalent        | Only `failure.kind` reaches the response; the cause is never read                                                                                                                      |
| L312 | ConditionalExpression `true` (`res.status >= 300`)                                                   | Equivalent        | Inside `!res.ok` a status below 300 would be 1xx, which a fetch Response cannot have (200 to 599), so the check reduces to `< 400` either way                                          |
| L317 | ArrowFunction `() => undefined`                                                                      | Equivalent        | `ErrorResponse.safeParse(undefined)` fails as `safeParse(null)` does                                                                                                                   |

#### `app/services/backend-client.server.ts` †

| Line       | Fault                                                                                                     | Class             | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| L80        | CallExpression (`clearTimeout` in `sleep`'s abort handler)                                                | Equivalent        | The handler resolves the promise; the leftover timer later calls `resolve` again, which does nothing on a settled promise                                                                                                                                   |
| L83        | ObjectLiteral `{}`; BooleanLiteral `false` (`once`)                                                       | Equivalent        | An AbortSignal fires `abort` once only                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| L110       | ConditionalExpression `false` (`!requestId`)                                                              | Equivalent        | With no request id the loop looks for keys starting "undefined ", and none exist: a read is only shared inside a request                                                                                                                                    |
| L153       | ConditionalExpression `false` (col 33, `opts.schema === null`)                                            | Equivalent        | A null schema means an empty route, whose shared answer is always undefined, so the first operand already returns                                                                                                                                           |
| L155       | ConditionalExpression `false`; BlockStatement `{}` (NoCo)                                                 | Not worth killing | Unreachable: the entry is shared only when `existing.schema === opts.schema`, so the body was already parsed by this same schema, and the deduped profile schema has no transforms, so a second parse cannot fail                                           |
| L177       | ConditionalExpression `true` (`method === "GET"` in the retry default)                                    | Equivalent        | L206 throws for any method other than GET before `retries` matters, so a write never retries whatever its default                                                                                                                                           |
| L180       | ConditionalExpression `false`; ConditionalExpression `true` (`method === "POST"`); EqualityOperator `!==` | Equivalent        | A write is never retried (L206), so the call makes one request; `callBackend` makes its own key for a POST given undefined and ignores the key on other methods, so each POST still sends exactly one key. The opt-out (`idempotencyKey: false`) has a test |
| L198; L253 | ConditionalExpression `false` (`!(err instanceof BackendError)`)                                          | Not worth killing | `callBackend` throws only BackendErrors or the caller's own abort reason; treated as a failure, a reason with no `kind` or 5xx `status` is not transient and is rethrown unchanged, as now                                                                  |
| L303       | OptionalChaining `opts.signal.reason`                                                                     | Equivalent        | `onCallerAbort` runs only from the signal's own listener or when `opts.signal?.aborted` is true, so the signal exists                                                                                                                                       |
| L305       | ObjectLiteral `{}`; BooleanLiteral `false` (`once`)                                                       | Equivalent        | An AbortSignal fires `abort` once only, and the listener is removed in `finally`                                                                                                                                                                            |
| L327       | BlockStatement `{}` (catch of `decodeURIComponent`)                                                       | Equivalent        | The assignment never happens when decoding throws, so `filename` stays null either way                                                                                                                                                                      |
| L339       | ObjectLiteral `{}` (`{ cause: err }`)                                                                     | Not worth killing | The cause is for logs only; the kind, endpoint and message callers read are unchanged                                                                                                                                                                       |
| L415; L470 | ArrayDeclaration `["Stryker was here"]` (NoCo, `a.details \|\| []`)                                       | Equivalent        | `details` is a required array in the account contract ("always present in the response"), so the fallback is never reached                                                                                                                                  |
| L531       | ObjectLiteral `{}` (`{ asOf: new Date() }`)                                                               | Equivalent        | With no `asOf`, `resolveUkTaxYear` and `buildFinancialPicture` both default to the current date, and the loader does not read `loaded.asOf`                                                                                                                 |

Going to pending also re-arms, but Enter is ignored while pending, and leaving pending re-arms in both versions; on mount `enterFired` is already false                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| L178; L179             | OptionalChaining (focus the button)                                                | Equivalent        | The buttons are rendered inside the same content, so the refs are set when Radix calls `onOpenAutoFocus`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| L225; L226             | ArrowFunction `() => undefined` (outside press and outside interaction handlers)   | Not worth killing | They only call `preventDefault` on Radix's own outside-press events. In the test DOM (happy-dom) Radix's deferred outside-press detection does not fire: a test pressing an outside button after Radix's listener tick passed with and without these handlers, so it was dropped rather than kept as a test that proves nothing. The behaviour (outside clicks never close the dialog) can only be checked in a real browser |

#### `src/patterns/MarginalRateChart/MarginalRateChart.tsx`

Most of what is left is drawing geometry: where the direct labels sit so they dodge the dashed lines and "Your pay", where the tooltip box sits, and the SVG coordinates and text anchors. Those faults move pixels, not information: every figure, label and threshold is still drawn and named, and the data table, the hover and focus targets and the tooltip text are tested. They are grouped below as Not worth killing (visual regression covers the drawing).

| L33           | MethodExpression (drop `.trim()`)                                     | Not worth killing | Differs only when the element's own computed `--app-header-offset` is whitespace alone; browsers return "" for an unset custom property, and the document fallback is covered by an existing test                         |
| L45           | BooleanLiteral `false` (initial `fits`)                               | Not worth killing | The first value is replaced by the measurement in the effect before the first paint matters; it only changes the server-rendered sticky class, a visual change                                                            |
| L54; L55; L58 | ConditionalExpression `true`; OptionalChaining (ResizeObserver guard) | Not worth killing | Differs only in a browser without `ResizeObserver`; every supported browser has it, and the window resize listener still re-measures                                                                                      |
| L57           | CallExpression (drop `removeEventListener`)                           | Not worth killing | A resize listener left after unmount only calls `setFits` on an unmounted component; no visible behaviour                                                                                                                 |
| L157          | OptionalChaining `renderControlsContainer(...)`                       | Equivalent        | The branch runs only when `custom` is true, which requires `renderControlsContainer !== undefined`                                                                                                                        |

### UI primitives (E41 M5, `packages/ui/src/primitives`, 21 November 2026)

| 13   | ConditionalExpression `trackingPointer \|\| typeof document === "undef...` -> `false`; LogicalOperator `trackingPointer \|\| typeof document === "undef...` -> `trackingPointer && typeof document === "undef...`; ConditionalExpression `typeof document === "undefined"` -> `false`; EqualityOperator `typeof document === "undefined"` -> `typeof document !== "undefined"` | Equivalent        | Registering the pointer listeners again only records the same target twice.                                                                             |
| 14   | BooleanLiteral `true` -> `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Equivalent        | Registering the pointer listeners again only records the same target twice.                                                                             |
| 18   | BooleanLiteral `true` -> `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Not worth killing | Capture only matters when a handler stops propagation of pointerdown before the document sees it; the Safari fallback itself is tested.                 |
| 19   | BooleanLiteral `true` -> `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Not worth killing | Capture only matters when a handler stops propagation of click before the document sees it; the Safari fallback itself is tested.                       |
| 27   | ConditionalExpression `el?.isConnected` -> `true`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Not worth killing | Only matters when the last pointer target was removed from the page before the dialog opened; landing on main or staying put are both acceptable there. |
| 40   | ConditionalExpression `typeof window === "undefined"` -> `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Equivalent        | window always exists in the browser; the check is for server rendering.                                                                                 |
| 43   | ConditionalExpression `box.width === 0 && box.height === 0` -> `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Equivalent        | A box of all zeros counts as in view, so no scroll happens either way.                                                                                  |
| 55   | ConditionalExpression `!hadTabIndex` -> `false`; CallExpression `main.setAttribute("tabindex", "-1");` -> `;`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Not worth killing | happy-dom focuses a main element with no tabindex, so the test DOM cannot tell; the tabindex kept on a main that had one is tested.                     |
| 68   | ArrayDeclaration `[]` -> `["Stryker was here"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Equivalent        | A constant dependency array: the effect or callback runs exactly as often either way.                                                                   |

### packages/ui: calculators, templates, hooks, charts and tools (E41 M5, ui-other)

Measured by the forced full run on commit 66c6fe131. Kills were checked with Stryker one file at a time for the calculators, and in one run for the hooks, charts and tools, on the worktree branch. Faults killed by tests added after those runs were checked by applying each fault by hand and seeing a test fail. Line numbers are those of 66c6fe131. The counts below are faults, not rows.
